L2123: **FDA Comment 9:** For patient (b)(6) explain the change in assessment by reviewer 2 from TP3 to TP4 since once PR has been assessed the next timepoint assessment should still be PR unless findings are consistent with PD. Please indicate if DOR for this patient was calculated based on PR date of TP3 or PR date of TP6.
L2124: **EMD Serono Response:** Change in assessment from TP3 to TP4 is associated with Reviewer 1; please see below an explanation for the change in the assessment.
L2125: Based on RECIST 1.1 guidelines and the independent review charter, all overall timepoint assessments are possible following a PR, depending on the changes in percentages with respect to Nadir and Baseline. Stable disease at a timepoint is assessed when there is neither sufficient regression with regard to Baseline nor progression when compared with the Nadir. Reviewer 1 assessed PR at TP3 with 32.1% decrease in sum of diameters of target lesions with respect to Baseline. However, Reviewer 1 assessed SD at TP4 with now only a 25.5% decrease in sum of diameters of target lesions with respect to Baseline and 9.7% increase with respect to Nadir (TP3). The 9.7% increase does not trigger PD, yet at the same time this case is no longer a valid PR assessment at that timepoint. Similarly, Reviewer 1 assessed SD at TP5 with now only 19.7% decrease in sum of diameters of target lesions with respect to Baseline and 18.2% increase with respect to Nadir (TP3). As such the SD assessment following the prior PR is correct based on the percentage changes in relation to Baseline and Nadir values as per RECIST 1.1 and the independent review charter-defined rules.
L2126: Duration of response (DOR) was calculated from the time of initial response (in the case of patient (b)(6) based on the IRC assessment at (b)(6) [TP3]) in accordance with the VISION Study Integrated Analysis Plan (IAP), RECIST 1.1 as well as the FDA guidance document 'Clinical Trial Endpoints for the Approval of Cancer Drugs and Biologics'.
L2127: **FDA Comment 10:** IR relating to all responders included in the primary analysis population(s): Provide details regarding any responders who started new anti-cancer treatment after response and specify how DOR has been calculated for these patients.
L2128: **EMD Serono Response:** Based on the VISION Study Integrated Analysis Plan (IAP), Section 14.2.2, DOR was determined as follows:
L2129: "The DOR will only be evaluated in subjects that have an objective response. DOR is the time from when the CR/PR (whichever is first) criteria are first met until PD or death due to any cause within 84 days of the last evaluable tumor assessment, whichever occurs first (see Eisenhauer et al., 2009). DOR data will be censored on the date of the last evaluable tumor assessment for subjects who do not have an event (PD or death) or for subjects with an event
L2130: more than 84 days after the last evaluable tumor assessment.” As per definition, DOR was determined irrespective of whether a subject received new anti-cancer therapy or not.
L2131: Twenty-one responders (based on IRC assessment) received new anti-cancer therapy (drug therapy or radiotherapy; no surgeries were reported) after response:
L2132: - For 11 out of 21 responders, progressive disease based on IRC assessment was reported before the patients started new anti-cancer therapy.
- For 10 out of 21 responders (see Table 1), the start of new anti-cancer therapy occurred before a DOR event (either PD based on IRC or death). Of those 10 responders:For 9 responders (see first 9 patients in Table 1), DOR was censored at the last evaluable IRC assessment, which occurred on a date before the start of new anticancer therapy. 7 of those patients were discontinued from study treatment due to PD as assessed by the Investigator and 2 were discontinued due to adverse events.
- 1 responder (b) (6) died within 1 month after start of subsequent radiotherapy. IRC did not assess a PD as reported by the Investigator at the last available tumor assessment, no further tumor assessments were performed. In accordance with the IAP, the death date was taken as the end of duration of response date (see details in Table 1).
L2133: For details on response-related data as well as on the first new anti-cancer therapy for all 21 responders based on IRC assessment in VISION cohort A who received new anti-cancer therapy, refer to NDA214096_FDA IR_23Dec20_VISION_JUL20_FUP_Additional7_20201228, Listing 16.2.5.2a (type, start date of subsequent therapy and IRC based tumor assessments) and Listing 16.2.5.6 (details on DOR-related data side-by-side with subsequent therapy dates).

L2156: Figure 1: Dose-response curve showing Tepotinib inhibition of HGF-induced c-Met phosphorylation in A549 lung cancer cells. The Y-axis is Phospho-MET (% control) and the X-axis is Concentration [µM]. Four conditions are compared: serum free, +10% mouse serum, +10% human serum, and serum free, 14h washout. The curve shows dose-dependent inhibition, with the highest inhibition observed in the serum free condition.
L2157: Figure 2 shows two dose-response curves illustrating the inhibition of cell proliferation by tepotinib. The left graph shows MKN-45 cells, where proliferation is significantly inhibited starting around 0.1 nM, with an IC50 of 6.2 nM. The right graph shows SNU-16 cells, where proliferation is inhibited only at much higher concentrations, with an IC50 of 2.8 µM. Both graphs plot % Control versus Concentration [µM] on a logarithmic scale.
L2158: Figure 3 displays six panels of soft agar colony formation assays. The top row shows results for cells treated with HGF (+HGF) and without HGF (-HGF). The bottom row shows results for cells treated with increasing concentrations of EMD1214063 (Tepotinib): 0.01 \muM, 0.1 \muM, 1 nM, 1 \muM, and 10 \muM. The control (-HGF) shows minimal colony formation. The +HGF control shows extensive colony formation. Tepotinib treatment, particularly at 1 nM and 1 \muM, significantly inhibits colony formation, especially in the presence of HGF.
(Excerpted from Study PSR-ONC-EMD1214063-002)
L2159: Figure 4 displays two dose-response curves showing the inhibition of S114 anchorage independent growth (% Control) versus Tepotinib concentration (\muM). The left panel shows inhibition when treatment is applied only on Day 8, and the right panel shows inhibition when treatment is applied on both Days 1 and 8. Both curves demonstrate dose-dependent inhibition, with the curve for treatment on Days 1 and 8 showing greater inhibition at lower concentrations.
(Excerpted from Study PSR-ONC-EMD1214063-002)

Two line graphs showing Tumor Volume (mm^3) versus Treatment Days (0 to 18) for the Hs746T gastric cancer model. The left graph shows daily administration (q3d) of EMD 1160879 (tepotinib free base) at 3 mg/kg and 6 mg/kg, resulting in tumor regression compared to the Vehicle group. L2162: Figure 7 shows two graphs illustrating tumor volume over time for MET-amplified EBC-1 (left) and MKN-45 (right) bearing mice treated with tepotinib. The left graph (EBC-1) shows tumor volume (mm^3) versus Treatment days (0 to 14). The Vehicle group shows increasing tumor volume. The EMD 1160879 6 mg/kg/qd group shows moderate growth. The EMD 1160879 25 mg/kg/qd group shows significant tumor regression. The right graph (MKN-45) shows tumor volume (mm^3) versus Experimental Days (0 to 12). The Vehicle group shows increasing tumor volume. The EMD1214063 25mg/kg/qd group shows moderate growth. The EMD1214063 200mg/kg/qd group shows significant tumor regression.
L2163: Figure 8 shows a graph illustrating tumor volume (mm^3) versus Experimental Days (0 to 21) for H441 tumors treated with daily oral tepotinib. The Vehicle group shows increasing tumor volume. The EMD1214063 25mg/kg/qd group shows moderate growth. The EMD1214063 200mg/kg/qd group shows significant tumor inhibition and partial regression.
L2164: Figure 9 displays two graphs showing the total compound concentration (ng/mL) over time (hours after last treatment) for mice bearing H441 tumors, comparing EMD1214063 at 25 mg/kg and 200 mg/kg doses across plasma, tumor, liver, and lung compartments.
The left graph (25 mg/kg) shows plasma concentration peaking around 4 hours (approx. 18,000 ng/mL) and rapidly declining. Tumor concentration peaks around 4 hours (approx. 1,500 ng/mL). Liver and lung concentrations are significantly lower, peaking around 4 hours (approx. 1,000 ng/mL and 500 ng/mL, respectively).
The right graph (200 mg/kg) shows plasma concentration peaking around 4 hours (approx. 70,000 ng/mL) and rapidly declining. Tumor concentration peaks around 4 hours (approx. 4,000 ng/mL). Liver and lung concentrations are significantly lower, peaking around 4 hours (approx. 2,000 ng/mL and 1,000 ng/mL, respectively).
(Excerpted from Study DOV-190-1-66-MFH)
L2165: Figure 10 is a line graph comparing tumor volume (mm^3) over time (days) for mice bearing human EBC-1 NSCLC tumors, comparing Vehicle, EMD 1160879 (tepotinib free base), and EMD 1214063 (tepotinib HCl) at two dose levels (6 mg/kg and 15 mg/kg, qd).
The Vehicle group shows rapid tumor growth, reaching approximately 600 mm^3 by day 14. The EMD 1160879 15 mg/kg group shows the strongest inhibition, with tumor volume remaining near 0 mm^3 throughout the 14-day treatment period. The EMD 1214063 15 mg/kg group also shows strong inhibition, with tumor volume remaining near 0 mm^3 throughout the 14-day treatment period. The EMD 1160879 6 mg/kg group shows moderate inhibition, reaching approximately 200 mm^3 by day 14. The EMD 1214063 6 mg/kg group shows moderate inhibition, reaching approximately 150 mm^3 by day 14.
(Excerpted from Study PSR-ONC-EMD1214063-004)

L2168: Figure 13 displays two box plots showing the Median tumor volume change [%] for LU5349 PDX model (d7 and d28) and LU5406 PDX model (d6 and d16). The Y-axis ranges from -100% to 1000%.
LU5349 PDX model: At d7, the Vehicle group shows a median tumor volume change of approximately 700%. At d28, the Vehicle group shows a median tumor volume change of approximately 300%. The Tepotinib group shows a median tumor volume change near 0% at both time points, indicating tumor regression.
LU5406 PDX model: At d6, the Vehicle group shows a median tumor volume change of approximately 700%. At d16, the Vehicle group shows a median tumor volume change of approximately 300%. The Tepotinib group shows a median tumor volume change near 0% at both time points, indicating tumor regression.
L2169: Line graph showing Tumor Volume (mm^3) Mean +/- SEM versus Treatment Days (0 to 10) for Vehicle, M506 (MSC2571109A-2) at 2, 10, and 50 mg/kg QD PO, and Tepotinib (MSC2156116J-16) at 200 mg/kg QD PO. Tumor volume increases significantly for M506 doses, while tepotinib shows minimal tumor growth.
L2170: Figure 15 is a line graph showing MET phosphorylation levels (pMET/total MET mean, ng/mg total protein) in tumor tissue over time (d18h, d20h, d24h, d48h) for various treatment groups. L2174: Figure 17 displays four plots showing the population simulation of phospho-MET inhibition relative to baseline over time (in weeks) for four different Tepotinib dosing regimens: 500 mg QD, 300 mg QD, 250 mg QD, and 200 mg QD. The Y-axis represents %phospho-MET relative to baseline, and the X-axis represents Time after the first dose (in weeks). The plots show the median inhibition (black line) and the 80% prediction interval (shaded green area). A dashed red line indicates the baseline level (5.0% relative to baseline). All four regimens show rapid initial inhibition, reaching approximately 5.0% relative to baseline within 0.5 weeks, and maintaining this level thereafter.
Figure 17: Population Simulation of phospho-MET Inhibition in Mixed-Solid Tumor Population. Four plots showing %phospho-MET relative to baseline versus Time after the first dose (in weeks) for Tepotinib 500 mg QD, Tepotinib 300 mg QD, Tepotinib 250 mg QD, and Tepotinib 200 mg QD. The median inhibition and 80% prediction interval are shown, stabilizing around 5.0% relative to baseline.

L2186: Figure 29 displays diagnostic plots for the final population PK model for tepotinib, organized into four rows and four columns, corresponding to four dose groups: 30-200 mg, 215-400 mg, 500 mg, and 700-1400 mg.
The top two rows show plots of Concentration (ng/mL) versus Prediction (ng/mL). The top row uses linear scales, and the bottom row uses logarithmic scales. The black line represents the line of unity, and the red line represents a smooth non-parametric trendline.
The bottom two rows show plots of CWRES (Conditional Weighted Residuals) versus Time after first dose (days) and Individual weighted residuals versus Individual prediction (ng/mL). The horizontal black line in the CWRES plots is the zero line, and the red line is a smooth non-parametric trendline.
L2187: Plot of CWRES versus Prediction (ng/mL). The horizontal black line is the zero line and the red line is a smooth non-parametric trendline.
L2188: Plot of CWRES versus Time after first dose (days). The horizontal black line is the zero line and the red line is a smooth non-parametric trendline.
L2189: Plot of Individual weighted residuals versus Individual prediction (ng/mL). The red line is a smooth non-parametric trendline.
L2190: Figure 30: pcVPC of tepotinib concentrations vs time since last dose. The plot shows Prediction Corrected Visual Predictive Check (pcVPC) results on a log-linear scale. The Y-axis is 'Prediction corrected concentration' (log scale, 0.1 to 1000) and the X-axis is 'Time since last dose (hour)' (0 to 175). The observed data (solid and dashed red lines) show the median, 5th, and 95th percentiles. The model predicted median (solid blue line) and the 5th and 95th percentiles (dashed blue lines) are shown. Shaded areas represent the 95% confidence intervals for the model predicted median (red) and the 5th and 95th percentiles (blue). The observed data generally falls within the model predicted confidence intervals, indicating a good fit.
L2191: Figure 31 displays 12 plots showing the Predicted Corrected Concentration (Y-axis, log-linear scale) versus Time since first dose (hour) (X-axis) for different studies (MS200095-0028, MS200095-0039, MS200095-0044, EMR200095-007, MS200095-0012, MS200095-0022, EMR200095-004, EMR200095-005, EMR200095-006, EMR200095-001, EMR200095-002, EMR200095-003). The plots show observed data (solid and dashed red lines) and model predictions (shaded red and blue areas).
L2192: Figure 32 displays six plots showing the prediction corrected visual predictive check (pcVPC) of tepotinib concentrations versus time since last dose (hour) for six different formulations. The plots are arranged in three rows and two columns. The Y-axis is 'Prediction corrected concentration' on a log-linear scale (1 to 1000). The X-axis is 'Time since last dose (hour)' (0 to 500). The plots are titled: Tablet formulation 1* (finely micronized), Tablet formulation 3, Tablet formulation 2, Tablet formulation 1 (micronized), Capsule formulation 1 (non-micronized), and Capsule formulation 2 (micronized). Each plot shows observed data (solid and dashed red lines representing median, 5th, and 95th percentiles) and model predictions (shaded red area for median, shaded blue areas for 5th and 95th percentiles).
